   274	def _parse_config(argv: list[str]) -> Config:
   275	    namespace = _argument_parser().parse_args(argv)
   276	    try:
   277	        root = Path(namespace.target).resolve()
   278	        root_is_dir = root.is_dir()
   279	    except (OSError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   280	        raise UsageError(f"TARGET_DIR resolve 불능: {namespace.target} ({exc})") from exc
   281	    bad_target_reason = checker_target.bc_shaped_target_reason(root)
   282	    if bad_target_reason is not None:
   283	        raise UsageError(bad_target_reason)
   284	    if not root_is_dir:
   285	        raise UsageError(f"디렉터리 아님 {root}")
   286	
   287	    issues: list[str] = []
   288	    profile = _one(
   289	        "--error-profile", namespace.error_profile, required=True, issues=issues
   290	    )
  2100	        if len(discriminator_candidates) != 1:
  2101	            analysis.append(
  2102	                f"{path}: {base_name} discriminator provenance 분석 불능"
  2103	            )
  2104	        else:
  2105	            discriminator_name, discriminator_statement, discriminator_bindings = (
  2106	                discriminator_candidates[0]
  2107	            )
  2108	            discriminator_fields[bc] = discriminator_name
  2109	            default = _field_default_expression(
  2110	                discriminator_statement,
  2111	                discriminator_bindings,
  2112	            )
  2113	            resolved_default = (
  2114	                _resolve_binding(default, discriminator_bindings)
  2115	                if default is not None
  2116	                else None
  2117	            )
  2118	            discriminator_defaults[bc] = _is_direct_enum_member(
  2119	                resolved_default,
  2120	                enum_origin,
  2121	            )
  2122	        known: set[str] = set()
  2123	        for node in classes:
  2124	            if node in base_nodes:
  2125	                continue
  2126	            bindings = timeline.before.get(id(node), {})
  2127	            direct = [
  2128	                _resolve_binding(base, bindings)
  2129	                for base in node.bases
  2130	            ]
  2131	            if len(node.bases) == 1 and direct[0] is not None and direct[0].origin == base_origin:
  2132	                known.add(f"{module}.{node.name}")
  2133	            elif any((_expression_name(base) or "").rsplit(".", 1)[-1] == base_name for base in node.bases):
  2134	                analysis.append(f"{path}:{node.lineno} prepared FrameworkErrorSchema base provenance 분석 불능")
  2135	        prepared[bc] = frozenset(known)
  2136	    return ErrorLanguage(
  2137	        frozenset(common_fields),
  2138	        frozenset(required_common_fields),
  2139	        frozenset(integer_common_fields),
  2140	        constructor_targets_by_key,
  2141	        certain_constructor_keys,
  2142	        tuple(sorted(set(constructor_key_issues))),
  2143	        discriminator_fields,
  2144	        discriminator_defaults,
  2145	        bases,
  2146	        prepared,
  2147	    )

  2150	def _node_symbol(node: ast.AST) -> str | None:
  2151	    """위반 심볼(U17) — node 가 안정 이름을 가진 경우만 채우고, 그 밖은 null."""
  2152	    if isinstance(node, (ast.ClassDef, ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
  2153	        return node.name
  2154	    if isinstance(node, ast.AnnAssign) and isinstance(node.target, ast.Name):
  2155	        return node.target.id
  2156	    return None

  2158	
  2159	def _append_finding(
  2160	    findings: list[Finding],
  2161	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  2162	    parsed: ParsedSource,
  2163	    node: ast.AST,
  2164	    category: str,
  2165	    *,
  2166	    rule: str | None = None,
  2167	    overlap_line: int | None = None,
  2168	    requires_static_error_shape: bool = False,
  2169	) -> None:
  2170	    lineno = getattr(node, "lineno", 1)
  2171	    key = (parsed.relative_path, lineno, category)
  2172	    if key in seen:
  2173	        return
  2174	    seen.add(key)
  2175	    source_lines = parsed.source.splitlines()
  2176	    shown = (
  2177	        source_lines[lineno - 1].strip()
  2178	        if 0 < lineno <= len(source_lines)
  2179	        else category
  2180	    )
  2181	    findings.append(
  2182	        Finding(
  2183	            parsed.relative_path,
  2184	            lineno,
  2185	            category,
  2186	            shown,
  2187	            rule=rule,
  2188	            symbol=_node_symbol(node),
  2189	            overlap_line=overlap_line,
  2190	            requires_static_error_shape=requires_static_error_shape,
  2191	        )
  2192	    )
  2193	
  2194	
  2195	def _iter_lexical_nodes(statements: list[ast.stmt]) -> Iterator[ast.AST]:
  2196	    """Yield one suite's nodes without entering a nested Python scope."""
  2197	    stack: list[ast.AST] = list(reversed(statements))
  2198	    while stack:
  2199	        node = stack.pop()
  2200	        yield node
  2201	        if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef, ast.Lambda)):
  2202	            continue
  2203	        children = list(ast.iter_child_nodes(node))
  2204	        stack.extend(reversed(children))
  2205	
  2206	
  2207	def _definition_expressions(node: ast.AST) -> list[ast.AST]:
  2208	    """Expressions evaluated outside a newly defined function/class body."""
  2209	    if isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
  2210	        expressions: list[ast.AST] = [*node.decorator_list]
  2211	        expressions.extend(node.args.defaults)
  2212	        expressions.extend(
  2213	            default for default in node.args.kw_defaults if default is not None
  2214	        )
  2215	        expressions.extend(
  2216	            argument.annotation
  2217	            for argument in _function_argument_nodes(node)
  2218	            if argument.annotation is not None
  2219	        )
  2220	        if node.returns is not None:
  2221	            expressions.append(node.returns)
  2222	        expressions.extend(getattr(node, "type_params", ()))
  2223	        return expressions
  2224	    if isinstance(node, ast.Lambda):
  2225	        expressions = [*node.args.defaults]
  3150	        valid = False
  3151	
  3152	    for statement in body[1:-1]:
  3153	        if not _header_assignment_valid(operation, statement, analysis):
  3154	            valid = False
  3155	    final = body[-1]
  3156	    status_call = (
  3157	        _exact_status_return(operation, final, error_name, language, analysis)
  3158	        if isinstance(final, ast.Return) and error_name is not None
  3159	        else None
  3160	    )
  3161	    if status_call is None:
  3162	        valid = False
  3163	    if valid and isinstance(value, ast.Call) and status_call is not None:
  3164	        allowed_error_calls.add(id(value))
  3165	        allowed_status_calls.add(id(status_call))
  3166	        return True
  3167	    _append_finding(
  3168	        findings,
  3169	        seen,
  3170	        operation.parsed,
  3171	        body[0],
  3172	        delegated_category if delegated else category,
  3173	        rule="#126" if delegated else None,  # 행19ⓐ/20ⓐ — «helper·factory 로 옮기지 않는다» 축자
  3174	        requires_static_error_shape=True,
  3175	    )
  3176	    return False
  3177	
  3178	
  3179	def _handler_type_names(node: ast.AST | None) -> list[ast.AST] | None:
  3180	    if node is None:
  3181	        return None
  3182	    if isinstance(node, ast.Tuple):
  3183	        return list(node.elts)
  3184	    return [node]
  3185	
  3186	
  3187	def _exception_origin_valid(operation: Operation, node: ast.AST) -> bool | None:
  3188	    if not isinstance(node, ast.Name):
  3189	        return False
  3190	    if node.id in {"Exception", "BaseException"}:
  3191	        return False
  3192	    if node.id in operation.local_names:
  3193	        return None
  3194	    binding = operation.body_bindings.get(node.id)
  3195	    definition_binding = operation.definition_bindings.get(node.id)
  3196	    prefix = f"application.{operation.owner_bc}."
  3197	    definition_was_own = (
  3198	        definition_binding is not None
  3199	        and definition_binding.kind == "symbol_import"
  3200	        and definition_binding.origin.startswith(prefix)
  3201	        and definition_binding.origin[len(prefix) :].startswith(
  3202	            ("application_layer.", "domain_layer.")
  3203	        )
  3204	    )
  3205	    if (
  3206	        definition_was_own
  3207	        and (
  3208	            binding is None
  3209	            or binding.kind != "symbol_import"
  3210	            or binding.origin != definition_binding.origin
  3211	        )
  3212	    ):
  3213	        # A body executes against the final module namespace.  If a name that
  3214	        # was directly imported when the function was defined is rebound later,
  3215	        # its runtime exception provenance can no longer be certified.
  3216	        return None
  3217	    if binding is None or binding.kind != "symbol_import":
  3218	        return False
  3219	    if not binding.origin.startswith(prefix):
  3220	        return False
  3221	    remainder = binding.origin[len(prefix) :]
  3222	    return remainder.startswith("application_layer.") or remainder.startswith("domain_layer.")
  3223	
  3224	
  3225	def _exception_origin_layer(operation: Operation, node: ast.AST) -> str | None:
  3226	    """catch type 의 층 판별(행7 분할 재료 — 귀속 매핑표 v2 U1·V5).
  3227	
  3228	    _exception_origin_valid 와 같은 binding 해석으로 자기 BC 의 domain_layer/
  3229	    application_layer 만 확정하고, 그 밖(로컬 섀도잉·비 symbol_import·타 출처)은
  3230	    None(층 미확정)으로 둔다."""
  3231	    if not isinstance(node, ast.Name):
  3232	        return None
  3233	    if node.id in operation.local_names:
  3234	        return None
  3235	    binding = operation.body_bindings.get(node.id)
  3236	    if binding is None or binding.kind != "symbol_import":
  3237	        return None
  3238	    prefix = f"application.{operation.owner_bc}."
  3239	    if not binding.origin.startswith(prefix):
  3240	        return None
  3241	    remainder = binding.origin[len(prefix) :]
  3242	    if remainder.startswith("domain_layer."):
  3243	        return "domain"
  3244	    if remainder.startswith("application_layer."):
  3245	        return "application"
  3246	    return None
  3247	
  3248	
  3249	def _handler_forwarding_attribution(
  3250	    operation: Operation,
  3251	    handler: ast.ExceptHandler,
  3252	) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
  3253	    """행7 분할(귀속 매핑표 v2 — U1·V5): catch 출처(provenance)로 category 를 나눈다.
  3254	
  3255	    ⓐ 도메인 예외 전달 = #474 «입구 파일은 도메인 예외를 타입으로만 쓴다» ·
  3256	    ⓑ 응용 예외 전달 = 08-04 계약(managed catch 규율 고유) ·
  3257	    층 미확정 잔여(혼합 tuple catch 등)는 계약 보수(기존 문면 유지 — U3 준용)."""
  3258	    layers: set[str] = set()
  3259	    for type_node in _handler_type_names(handler.type) or []:
  3260	        layer = _exception_origin_layer(operation, type_node)
  3261	        if layer is None:
  3262	            return "caught exception forwarding forbidden", None
  3263	        layers.add(layer)
  3264	    if layers == {"domain"}:
  3265	        return "caught domain exception forwarding forbidden", "#474"
  3266	    if layers == {"application"}:
  3267	        return "caught application exception forwarding forbidden", None
  3268	    return "caught exception forwarding forbidden", None
  3269	
  3270	
  3271	def _caught_exception_forwarded(handler: ast.ExceptHandler) -> bool:
  3272	    if not handler.name:
  3273	        return False
  3274	
  3275	    def contains_caught_name(root: ast.AST) -> bool:
  3276	        return any(
  3277	            isinstance(candidate, ast.Name) and candidate.id == handler.name
  3278	            for candidate in _iter_evaluated_nodes(root)
  3279	        )
  3280	
  3281	    for statement in handler.body:
  3282	        for node in _iter_evaluated_nodes(statement):
  3283	            if not isinstance(node, ast.Call):
  3284	                continue
  3285	            values = [*node.args, *(keyword.value for keyword in node.keywords)]
  3286	            if any(contains_caught_name(value) for value in values):
  3287	                return True
  3288	    return False
  3289

  3291	def _try_root_call(statement: ast.stmt) -> ast.Call | None:
  3292	    if not isinstance(statement, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign, ast.Expr)):
  3293	        return None
  3294	    value = _statement_value(statement)
  3295	    return value if isinstance(value, ast.Call) else None

  3298	def _analyze_try(
  3299	    operation: Operation,
  3300	    node: ast.Try,
  3301	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  3302	    analysis: list[str],
  3303	    findings: list[Finding],
  3304	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  3305	    allowed_error_calls: set[int],
  3306	    allowed_status_calls: set[int],
  3307	) -> None:
  3308	    if node.orelse or node.finalbody:
  3309	        _append_finding(
  3310	            findings,
  3311	            seen,
  3312	            operation.parsed,
  3313	            node,
  3314	            "managed try cannot have else/finally",
  3315	        )
  3316	    if len(node.body) != 1 or _try_root_call(node.body[0]) is None:
  3317	        _append_finding(
  3318	            findings,
  3319	            seen,
  3320	            operation.parsed,
  3321	            node,
  3322	            "managed try body must be one root-call statement",
  3323	        )
  3324	    if any(isinstance(candidate, ast.Raise) for candidate in _iter_lexical_nodes(node.body)):
  3325	        # 행3(#125) — «입구에 로직을 두지 않는다» 확정 위반. tree ⓓ#125 겹침은
  3326	        # route 함수 def 행 좌표로 선점 억제(overlap 절).
  3327	        _append_finding(
  3328	            findings,
  3329	            seen,
  3330	            operation.parsed,
  3331	            node,
  3332	            "raise inside managed try",
  3333	            rule="#125",
  3334	            overlap_line=operation.node.lineno,
  3335	        )
  3336	    for handler in node.handlers:
  3337	        type_nodes = _handler_type_names(handler.type)
  3338	        if type_nodes is None:
  3339	            # 행4(#62) — bare `except:` 는 catch-all 의 극단형.
  3340	            _append_finding(
  3341	                findings,
  3342	                seen,
  3343	                operation.parsed,
  3344	                handler,
  3345	                "bare catch forbidden",
  3346	                rule="#62",
  3347	                overlap_line=handler.lineno,
  3348	            )
  3349	        else:
  3350	            for type_node in type_nodes:
  6850	                finding_keys.append(None)
  6851	            if routes and is_controller:
  6852	                question = "입구가 변환·1회 호출을 넘어 로직을 갖는가(그러면 유스케이스로 내린다)?"
  6853	                for sub in node.body:
  6854	                    if isinstance(sub, (_ast.For, _ast.While)):
  6855	                        sub_where = f"{rel}:{sub.lineno}"
  6856	                        candidates.add("#125", sub_where, "라우트 메서드 안 루프", question)
  6857	                        candidate_keys.append(("#125", rel.as_posix(), node.lineno))
  6858	                        break
  6859	                    if isinstance(sub, _ast.If) and not _is_exc_mapping_if(sub, domain_names):
  6860	                        sub_where = f"{rel}:{sub.lineno}"
  6861	                        candidates.add("#125", sub_where, "라우트 메서드 안 분기", question)
  6862	                        candidate_keys.append(("#125", rel.as_posix(), node.lineno))
  6863	                        break
  6864	        elif isinstance(node, _ast.ExceptHandler):
  6865	            caught = node.type
  6866	            caught_names: list[str] = []
  6867	            if isinstance(caught, _ast.Tuple):
  6868	                caught_names = [getattr(e, "id", getattr(e, "attr", "")) for e in caught.elts]
  6869	            elif caught is not None:
  6870	                caught_names = [getattr(caught, "id", getattr(caught, "attr", ""))]
  6871	            if caught is None or set(caught_names) & _CATCH_ALL_NAMES:
  6872	                where = f"{rel}:{node.lineno}"
  6873	                msg = "`except Exception`/bare — 폴백은 도메인·응용 base 단위 catch 로 한정한다(base 는 상한이다 — code-json managed controller 는 concrete/구체 tuple 만 catch 한다: ninja §6.2)"
  6874	                findings.add("#62", where, msg)
  6875	                finding_keys.append(("#62", rel.as_posix(), node.lineno))
  6876	            if node.name and set(caught_names) & domain_names:
  6877	                for sub in _ast.walk(node):
  6878	                    if isinstance(sub, _ast.Name) and sub.id == node.name and isinstance(sub.ctx, _ast.Load):
  6879	                        sub_where = f"{rel}:{sub.lineno}"
  6880	                        msg = f"도메인 예외를 `as {node.name}` 로 묶어 참조했다 — 입구 파일은 도메인 예외를 «타입»으로만 쓴다"
  6881	                        # locator 는 참조 행이지만 사건 좌표는 handler 행이다
  6882	                        # (overlap 절 — locator 가 달라도 같은 incident).
  6883	                        findings.add("#474", sub_where, msg)
  6884	                        finding_keys.append(("#474", rel.as_posix(), node.lineno))
  6885	                        break
  6886

  6888	def _is_exc_mapping_if(node: _ast.If, domain_names: set[str]) -> bool:
  6889	    """`if isinstance(exc, DomainError):` 꼴 매핑 분기는 #126 의 정상 형태라 후보에서 뺀다."""
  6890	    for sub in _ast.walk(node.test):
  6891	        if isinstance(sub, _ast.Call) and getattr(sub.func, "id", "") == "isinstance":
  6892	            return True
  6893	    return False

  6896	def _tree_slice2(
  6897	    root: Path, bcs: list[Path]
  6898	) -> "tuple[Findings, Candidates, list[tuple[str, str, int] | None], list[tuple[str, str, int] | None]]":
  6899	    # 구조화 엔트리 수집 — 방출은 호출측 emit_all 이 한 순서로 수행한다(출력 계약 v2).
  6900	    # keys 두 목록은 엔트리와 같은 순서·같은 길이다(선점 억제 zip 좌표 — 억제 비대상
  6901	    # 사이트는 None).
  6902	    findings: Findings = Findings(defer=True)
  6903	    candidates: Candidates = Candidates(defer=True)
  6904	    finding_keys: "list[tuple[str, str, int] | None]" = []
  6905	    candidate_keys: "list[tuple[str, str, int] | None]" = []
  6906	    for bc in bcs:
  6907	        app_layer = bc / "application_layer"
  6908	        app_areas = (
  6909	            {p.name for p in app_layer.iterdir() if p.is_dir() and p.name not in ("port", "domain_bypass_query", "unit_of_work")}
  6910	            if app_layer.is_dir()
  6911	            else None
  6912	        )
  6913	        for driving in _TREE_DRIVING:
  6914	            api = bc / driving / "api"
  6915	            if api.is_dir():
  6916	                for area in sorted(p for p in api.iterdir() if p.is_dir()):
  6917	                    if area.name in ("webhook", "__pycache__") or area.name.startswith("."):
  6918	                        continue
  6919	                    area_rel = area.relative_to(root)
  6920	                    area_where = f"{area_rel}/"
  6921	                    if area.name.lower() in _TECH_DIR_TOKENS:
  6922	                        msg = f"`api/` 의 1차 축은 `<area>/` 다 — 기술 폴더(`{area.name}/`)를 만들지 않는다"
  6923	                        findings.add("#120", area_where, msg)

  6969	def _code_overlap_keys(findings: "list[Finding]") -> "set[tuple[str, str, int]]":
  6970	    """code 레인 실발화 사건의 tree 대응 좌표(귀속 매핑표 v2 overlap 절) —
  6971	    Finding.overlap_line 이 채워진 #62/#474/#125 만 억제 키가 된다."""
  6972	    keys: "set[tuple[str, str, int]]" = set()
  6973	    for finding in findings:
  6974	        if finding.rule in _OVERLAP_RULES and finding.overlap_line is not None:
  6975	            keys.add((finding.rule, finding.path.as_posix(), finding.overlap_line))
  6976	    return keys

  6979	def _suppress_overlapped_tree(
  6980	    tree: "Findings | Candidates",
  6981	    tree_keys: "list[tuple[str, str, int] | None]",
  6982	    code_keys: "set[tuple[str, str, int]]",
  6983	) -> "Findings | Candidates":
  6984	    """tree↔code 동일 사건 선점 억제(귀속 매핑표 v2 overlap 절 — U13·U14).
  6985	
  6986	    code 레인이 같은 사건(#62·#474 handler 행 · ⓓ#125 route 함수 def 행)을 적중한
  6987	    대상의 tree 사이트는 라인·레코드 모두 방출하지 않는다(1건 대표 — 더 정밀한
  6988	    판정이 이긴다. 대상 밖·code 미발화에서는 tree 단독 그대로)."""
  6989	    kept = type(tree)(tree.checker, defer=True)
  6990	    for entry, key in zip(tree.entries, tree_keys):
  6991	        if key is not None and key in code_keys:
  6992	            continue
  6993	        kept.entries.append(entry)
  6994	        kept.append(entry.line)
  6995	    return kept

  7027	                file=sys.stderr,

  7030	
  7031	    # 표준 트리 슬라이스 — 프로필과 무관하게 먼저 본다(옛 auto 무동작 = fail-open 차단).
  7032	    bcs = _tree_bcs2(config.root)
  7033	    if not any((bc / d).is_dir() for bc in bcs for d in _TREE_DRIVING) and _tree_adopted2(bcs):
  7034	        # 대상-0 가드 — 라인 = msg 원문(무들여쓰기 byte 보존) + rule=«대상0» 센티널

  7038	        )
  7039	        emit_all(guard, printer=print, indent="")
  7040	        return 2
  7041	    # --anchor 미지정이면 현행 그대로 각 슬라이스에서 즉시 exit 2 — 지정 시에만
  7042	    # 슬라이스 진단을 모아 마지막에 판정 차분(anchor_diff)으로 exit 를 정한다.
  7043	    collected: list[str] = []
  7044	    pending_analysis: list[str] = []
  7045	    tree_findings, tree_candidates, tree_keys, candidate_keys = _tree_slice2(config.root, bcs)

  3527	        name: set() for name in _annotated_error_parameters(operation, language)
  3528	    }
  3529	    for node in _iter_lexical_nodes(operation.node.body):
  3530	        if not isinstance(node, (ast.Assign, ast.AnnAssign)):
  3531	            continue
  3532	        name = _simple_assignment_target(node)
  3533	        value = _statement_value(node)
  3534	        if name is None or not isinstance(value, ast.Call):
  3535	            continue
  3536	        if _known_constructor(operation, value, language, analysis) is not None:
  3537	            assignments.setdefault(name, set()).add(id(value))
  3538	    return assignments
  3539	
  3540	
  3541	def _status_is_error_mapping(
  3542	    operation: Operation,
  3543	    call: ast.Call,
  3544	    error_assignments: dict[str, set[int]],
  3545	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  3546	    analysis: list[str],
  3547	) -> bool:
  3548	    if not _status_call(operation, call, analysis) or len(call.args) < 2:
  3549	        return False
  3550	    second = call.args[1]
  3551	    if isinstance(second, ast.Name) and second.id in error_assignments:
  3552	        return True
  3553	    return isinstance(second, ast.Call) and _known_constructor(
  3554	        operation, second, language, analysis
  3555	    ) is not None
  3556	
  3557	
  3558	def _literal_error_status_call(
  3559	    operation: Operation,
  3560	    call: ast.Call,
  3561	    analysis: list[str],
  3562	) -> bool:
  3563	    if not _status_call(operation, call, analysis) or len(call.args) < 2:
  3564	        return False
  3565	    status = call.args[0]
  3566	    return (
  3567	        isinstance(status, ast.Constant)
  3568	        and isinstance(status.value, int)
  3569	        and 400 <= status.value <= 599
  3570	    )
  3571	
  3572	
  3573	def _branch_has_error_behavior(
  3574	    operation: Operation,
  3575	    statements: list[ast.stmt],
  3576	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  3577	    error_assignments: dict[str, set[int]],
  3578	    analysis: list[str],
  3579	) -> bool:
  3580	    for node in _iter_lexical_nodes(statements):
  3581	        if isinstance(node, ast.Raise) or _raw_error_terminal(operation, node):
  3582	            return True
  3583	        if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
  3584	            if _known_constructor(operation, node, language, analysis) is not None:
  3585	                return True
  3586	            if _status_is_error_mapping(
  3587	                operation, node, error_assignments, language, analysis
  3588	            ):
  3589	                return True
  3590	            if _literal_error_status_call(operation, node, analysis):
  3591	                return True
  3592	    return False
  3593	
  3594	
  3595	def _candidate_branch(
  3596	    operation: Operation,
  3597	    node: ast.AST,
  3598	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  3599	    error_assignments: dict[str, set[int]],
  3600	    analysis: list[str],
  3601	) -> bool:
  3602	    if isinstance(node, ast.If):
  3603	        if _result_test_name(node.test) is not None:
  3604	            return True
  3605	        return _branch_has_error_behavior(
  3606	            operation,
  3607	            [*node.body, *node.orelse],
  3608	            language,
  3609	            error_assignments,
  3610	            analysis,
  3611	        )
  3612	    if MATCH is not None and isinstance(node, MATCH):
  3613	        bodies = [statement for case in node.cases for statement in case.body]
  3614	        return _branch_has_error_behavior(
  3615	            operation, bodies, language, error_assignments, analysis
  3616	        )
  3617	    return False
  3618	
  3619	
  3620	def _call_assignment(statement: ast.stmt) -> tuple[str, ast.Call] | None:
  3621	    name = _simple_assignment_target(statement)
  3622	    value = _statement_value(statement)
  3623	    if name is None or not isinstance(value, ast.Call):
  3624	        return None
  3625	    return name, value
  3626	
  3627	
  3628	def _try_assignment(statement: ast.stmt) -> tuple[str, ast.Call] | None:
  3629	    if not isinstance(statement, ast.Try) or len(statement.body) != 1:
  3630	        return None
  3631	    return _call_assignment(statement.body[0])
  3632	
  3633	
  3634	def _analyze_results(
  3635	    operation: Operation,
  3636	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  3637	    analysis: list[str],
  3638	    findings: list[Finding],
  3639	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  3640	    allowed_error_calls: set[int],
  3641	    allowed_status_calls: set[int],
  3642	) -> None:
  3643	    body = _without_docstrings(operation.node.body)
  3644	    error_assignments = _known_error_assignments(operation, language, analysis)
  3645	    approved_branches: set[int] = set()
  3646	
  3647	    for index in range(len(body) - 1):
  3648	        assignment = _call_assignment(body[index])
  3649	        branch = body[index + 1]
  3650	        if assignment is None or not isinstance(branch, ast.If):
  3651	            continue
  3652	        if _known_constructor(operation, assignment[1], language, analysis) is not None:
  3653	            # A local FrameworkErrorSchema construction is mapping behavior, not the
  3654	            # application-call assignment that can causally own a Result arm.
  3655	            continue
  3656	        result_name, _ = assignment
  3657	        if not _candidate_branch(
  3658	            operation, branch, language, error_assignments, analysis
  3659	        ):
  3660	            continue
  3661	        if not _supported_result_test(operation, branch.test, result_name, analysis):
  3662	            analysis.append(
  3663	                f"{operation.parsed.relative_path}:{branch.lineno} Result candidate predicate 분석 불능"
  3664	            )
  3665	            continue
  3666	        approved_branches.add(id(branch))
  3667	        _validate_mapping_body(
  3668	            operation,
  3669	            branch.body,
  3670	            language,
  3671	            analysis,
  3672	            findings,
  3673	            seen,
  3674	            allowed_error_calls,
  3675	            allowed_status_calls,
  3676	            "Result arm must directly construct FrameworkErrorSchema and return Status",
  3677	            "Result arm delegates error construction to helper/factory/serializer",
  3678	        )
  3679	
  3680	    for index, statement in enumerate(body):
  3681	        if not isinstance(statement, ast.If):
  3682	            continue
  3683	        if id(statement) in approved_branches:
  3684	            continue
  3685	        if not _candidate_branch(
  3970	                        and keyword.arg in keywords
  3971	                        and error_value(keyword.value)
  3972	                    ):
  3973	                        proven.setdefault(target_key, set()).add(keyword.arg)
  3974	
  3975	            def inspect_expression(root: ast.AST, error_names: set[str]) -> None:
  3976	                for node in _iter_evaluated_nodes(root):
  3977	                    if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
  3978	                        inspect_call(node, error_names)
  3979	
  3980	            def merge_error_names(states: list[set[str]]) -> set[str]:
  3981	                return {name for state in states for name in state}
  3982	
  3983	            def scan_simple(statement: ast.stmt, incoming: set[str]) -> set[str]:
  3984	                error_names = set(incoming)
  3985	                inspect_expression(statement, error_names)
  3986	                error_names.difference_update(_statement_bound_names(statement))
  3987	                target = _simple_assignment_target(statement)
  3988	                value = _statement_value(statement)
  3989	                if target is not None and isinstance(value, ast.Call):
  3990	                    if _known_constructor(caller, value, language, analysis) is not None:
  3991	                        error_names.add(target)
  3992	                return error_names
  3993	
  3994	            def scan_suite(
  3995	                statements: list[ast.stmt], incoming: set[str]
  3996	            ) -> set[str]:
  3997	                error_names = set(incoming)
  3998	                for statement in statements:
  3999	                    if isinstance(
  4000	                        statement,
  4001	                        (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef),
  4002	                    ):
  4003	                        error_names.difference_update(
  4004	                            _statement_bound_names(statement)
  4005	                        )
  4006	                        continue
  4007	                    if isinstance(statement, ast.If):
  4008	                        inspect_expression(statement.test, error_names)
  4009	                        truth = _literal_truth(statement.test)
  4010	                        if truth is True:
  4011	                            error_names = scan_suite(statement.body, error_names)
  4012	                        elif truth is False:
  4013	                            error_names = scan_suite(statement.orelse, error_names)
  4014	                        else:
  4015	                            error_names = merge_error_names(
  4016	                                [
  4017	                                    scan_suite(statement.body, error_names),
  4018	                                    scan_suite(statement.orelse, error_names),
  4019	                                ]
  4020	                            )
  4021	                        continue
  4022	                    if isinstance(statement, ast.While):
  4023	                        inspect_expression(statement.test, error_names)
  4024	                        body_out = scan_suite(statement.body, error_names)
  4025	                        error_names = merge_error_names(
  4026	                            [
  4027	                                error_names,
  4028	                                scan_suite(statement.orelse, error_names),
  4029	                                scan_suite(statement.orelse, body_out),
  4030	                            ]
  4031	                        )
  4032	                        continue
  4033	                    if isinstance(statement, (ast.For, ast.AsyncFor)):
  4034	                        inspect_expression(statement.iter, error_names)
  4035	                        body_in = set(error_names)
  4036	                        body_in.difference_update(_target_names(statement.target))
  4037	                        body_out = scan_suite(statement.body, body_in)
  4038	                        error_names = merge_error_names(
  4039	                            [
  4040	                                scan_suite(statement.orelse, error_names),
  4041	                                scan_suite(statement.orelse, body_out),
  4042	                            ]
  4043	                        )
  4044	                        continue
  4045	                    if isinstance(statement, (ast.With, ast.AsyncWith)):
  4046	                        body_in = set(error_names)
  4047	                        for item in statement.items:
  4048	                            inspect_expression(item.context_expr, body_in)
  4049	                            if item.optional_vars is not None:
  4050	                                body_in.difference_update(
  4250	                bindings.pop(name, None)
  4251	        return bindings
  4252	
  4253	    scan_suite(parsed.tree.body, {}, scope_kind="module")

  4256	def _helper_function_facts(
  4257	    function: Operation,
  4258	    language: ErrorLanguage,
  4259	    initial_error_names: set[str],
  4260	    analysis: list[str],
  4261	) -> HelperFacts:
  4262	    facts = HelperFacts()
  4263	
  4264	    def inspect_expression(root: ast.AST, error_names: set[str]) -> None:
  4265	        for node in _iter_evaluated_nodes(root):
  4266	            if isinstance(node, ast.Call):
  4267	                constructor = _known_constructor(function, node, language, analysis)
  4268	                if constructor is not None:
  4269	                    facts.has_error_constructor = True
  4270	                    if constructor[0] == "prepared":
  4271	                        facts.has_prepared = True
  4272	                if _exception_identification(function, node, analysis):
  4273	                    facts.has_exception_test = True
  4274	                if _raw_response_call(function, node) and (
  4275	                    _model_dump_receivers(node) & error_names
  4276	                ):
  4277	                    facts.serializer_node = node
  4278	
  4279	    def merge_error_names(states: list[set[str]]) -> set[str]:
  4280	        return {name for state in states for name in state}
  4281	
  4282	    def scan_simple(statement: ast.stmt, incoming: set[str]) -> set[str]:
  4283	        error_names = set(incoming)
  4284	        value = _statement_value(statement)
  4285	        if value is not None:
  4286	            inspect_expression(value, error_names)
  4287	        elif not isinstance(
  4288	            statement,
  4289	            (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef),
  4290	        ):
  4291	            inspect_expression(statement, error_names)
  4292	
  4293	        bound = _statement_bound_names(statement)
  4294	        error_names.difference_update(bound)
  4295	        target = _simple_assignment_target(statement)
  4296	        if target is not None and isinstance(value, ast.Call):
  4297	            if _known_constructor(function, value, language, analysis) is not None:
  4298	                error_names.add(target)
  4299	        return error_names
  4300	
  4301	    def scan_suite(statements: list[ast.stmt], incoming: set[str]) -> set[str]:
  4302	        error_names = set(incoming)
  4303	        for statement in statements:
  4304	            if isinstance(statement, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)):
  4305	                error_names.difference_update(_statement_bound_names(statement))
  4306	                continue
  4307	            if isinstance(statement, ast.If):
  4308	                inspect_expression(statement.test, error_names)
  4309	                truth = _literal_truth(statement.test)
  4310	                if truth is True:
  4311	                    error_names = scan_suite(statement.body, error_names)
  4312	                elif truth is False:
  4313	                    error_names = scan_suite(statement.orelse, error_names)
  4314	                else:
  4315	                    error_names = merge_error_names(
  4316	                        [
  4317	                            scan_suite(statement.body, error_names),
  4318	                            scan_suite(statement.orelse, error_names),
  4319	                        ]
  4320	                    )
  4321	                continue
  4322	            if isinstance(statement, ast.While):
  4323	                inspect_expression(statement.test, error_names)
  4324	                body_out = scan_suite(statement.body, error_names)
  4325	                error_names = merge_error_names(
  4326	                    [
  4327	                        error_names,
  4328	                        scan_suite(statement.orelse, error_names),
  4329	                        scan_suite(statement.orelse, body_out),
  4330	                    ]
  4331	                )
  4332	                continue
  4333	            if isinstance(statement, (ast.For, ast.AsyncFor)):
  4334	                inspect_expression(statement.iter, error_names)
  4335	                body_in = set(error_names)
  4336	                body_in.difference_update(_target_names(statement.target))
  4337	                body_out = scan_suite(statement.body, body_in)
  4338	                error_names = merge_error_names(
  4339	                    [
  4340	                        scan_suite(statement.orelse, error_names),
  4341	                        scan_suite(statement.orelse, body_out),
  4342	                    ]
  4343	                )
  4344	                continue
  4345	            if isinstance(statement, (ast.With, ast.AsyncWith)):
  4346	                body_in = set(error_names)
  4347	                for item in statement.items:
  4348	                    inspect_expression(item.context_expr, body_in)
  4349	                    if item.optional_vars is not None:
  4350	                        body_in.difference_update(_target_names(item.optional_vars))
  4351	                error_names = scan_suite(statement.body, body_in)
  4352	                continue
  4353	            if isinstance(statement, ast.Try):
  4354	                normal = scan_suite(statement.body, error_names)
  4355	                outcomes = [scan_suite(statement.orelse, normal)]
  4356	                for handler in statement.handlers:
  4357	                    handler_in = set(error_names)
  4358	                    if handler.type is not None:
  4359	                        inspect_expression(handler.type, handler_in)
  4360	                    if handler.name:
  4361	                        handler_in.discard(handler.name)
  4362	                    outcomes.append(scan_suite(handler.body, handler_in))
  4363	                error_names = scan_suite(
  4364	                    statement.finalbody,
  4365	                    merge_error_names(outcomes),
  4366	                )
  4367	                continue
  4368	            error_names = scan_simple(statement, error_names)
  4369	        return error_names
  4370	
  4371	    scan_suite(function.node.body, initial_error_names)
  4372	    return facts
  4373	
  4374	
  4375	def _nested_functions(
  6460	                        True
  6461	                        if MATCH_AS is not None
  6462	                        and isinstance(case.pattern, MATCH_AS)
  6463	                        and case.pattern.pattern is None
  6464	                        else None
  6465	                    )
  6466	                )
  6467	                guard_truth = (
  6468	                    True if case.guard is None else _literal_truth(case.guard)
  6469	                )
  6470	                if match is False or guard_truth is False:
  6471	                    continue
  6472	                case_bindings = dict(bindings)
  6473	                case_candidates = dict(candidates)
  6474	                for candidate in ast.walk(case.pattern):
  6475	                    name = getattr(candidate, "name", None)
  6476	                    if isinstance(name, str):
  6477	                        case_bindings.pop(name, None)
  6478	                        case_candidates.pop(name, None)
  6479	                if case.guard is not None:
  6480	                    inspect_expression(

  6482	                    )

  6485	                        source,
  6486	                        case.body,

  6488	                        case_candidates,
  6489	                    )
  6490	                )
  6491	                if match is True and guard_truth is True:
  6492	                    exhaustive = True
  6493	                    break
  6494	            if not exhaustive:
  6495	                outcomes.append((bindings, candidates, "normal"))
  6496	            return coalesce_outcomes(outcomes)
  6497	        if isinstance(
  6498	            statement,
  6499	            (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef),
  6500	        ):
  6501	            for expression in _definition_expressions(statement):

  6503	            for decorator in statement.decorator_list:
  6504	                record_implicit_decorator_call(

  6506	                )

  6512	                    and isinstance(target, (ast.Tuple, ast.List))
  6513	                    for target in getattr(statement, "targets", ())
  6514	                )
  6515	                inspect_expression(
  6516	                    value,
  6517	                    bindings,
  6518	                    candidates,
  6519	                    consume_generator=consume,
  6520	                )
  6521	            elif not isinstance(
  6522	                statement, (ast.Pass, ast.Global, ast.Nonlocal)
  6523	            ):
  6524	                inspect_expression(statement, bindings, candidates)
  6525	        advance_state(source, statement, bindings, candidates)
  6526	        control = (
  6527	            "return"
  6528	            if isinstance(statement, ast.Return)
  6529	            else "raise"
  6530	            if isinstance(statement, ast.Raise)
  6531	            else "break"
  6532	            if isinstance(statement, ast.Break)
  6533	            else "continue"
  6534	            if isinstance(statement, ast.Continue)
  6535	            else "normal"
  6536	        )
  6537	        return [(bindings, candidates, control)]
  6538	
  6539	    def scan_suite(
  6540	        source: ParsedSource,
  6541	        statements: list[ast.stmt],
  6542	        incoming: dict[str, Binding],
  6543	        incoming_candidates: CandidateState,
  6544	    ) -> list[Outcome]:
  6545	        outcomes: list[Outcome] = [
  6546	            (dict(incoming), dict(incoming_candidates), "normal")
  6547	        ]
  6548	        for statement in statements:
  6549	            updated: list[Outcome] = []
  6550	            for bindings, candidates, control in outcomes:
  6551	                if control != "normal":
  6552	                    updated.append((bindings, candidates, control))
  6553	                    continue
  6554	                updated.extend(
  6555	                    scan_statement(
  6556	                        source,
  6557	                        statement,
  6558	                        bindings,
  6559	                        candidates,
  6560	                    )
  6561	                )
  6562	            outcomes = coalesce_outcomes(updated)
  6563	            if not any(control == "normal" for _, _, control in outcomes):
  6564	                break
  6565	        return outcomes
  6566	
  6567	    for path in caller_paths:
  6568	        source = parsed.get(path)
  6569	        if source is None:
  6570	            continue
  6571	        module_outcomes = scan_suite(source, source.tree.body, {}, {})
  6572	        module_states = [
  6573	            (bindings, candidates)
  6574	            for bindings, candidates, control in module_outcomes
  6575	            if control == "normal"
  6576	        ]
  6577	        if not module_states:
  6578	            module_states = [
  6579	                (bindings, candidates)
  6580	                for bindings, candidates, _ in module_outcomes
  6581	            ]
  6582	        module_bindings, module_candidates = merge_states(module_states)
  6583	        for operation in operations_by_path.get(path, []):
  6584	            function_bindings = dict(module_bindings)
  6585	            function_candidates = dict(module_candidates)
  6586	            for name in _function_local_names(operation.node):
  6587	                function_bindings.pop(name, None)
  6588	                function_candidates.pop(name, None)
  6589	            scan_suite(
  6590	                source,
  6591	                operation.node.body,
  6592	                function_bindings,
  6593	                function_candidates,
  6594	            )
  6595	    return {
  6596	        path: {
  6597	            function_id: tuple(invocations)
  6598	            for function_id, invocations in functions.items()
  6599	        }
  6600	        for path, functions in entries.items()
  6601	    }

  6604	def _semantic_findings(
  6605	    config: Config,
  7030	
  7031	    # 표준 트리 슬라이스 — 프로필과 무관하게 먼저 본다(옛 auto 무동작 = fail-open 차단).
  7032	    bcs = _tree_bcs2(config.root)
  7033	    if not any((bc / d).is_dir() for bc in bcs for d in _TREE_DRIVING) and _tree_adopted2(bcs):
  7034	        # 대상-0 가드 — 라인 = msg 원문(무들여쓰기 byte 보존) + rule=«대상0» 센티널
  7035	        # 레코드. #74 는 달지 않는다(소유자 checker_lint — rule-owner-map).
  7036	        guard: Findings = zero_target_guard(
  7037	            "[check-api-error-controller-contract] blocker: 채택 신호는 있는데 driving 층이 0건이다 — 조용한 무동작을 금지한다(#74)"
  7038	        )
  7039	        emit_all(guard, printer=print, indent="")
  7040	        return 2
  7041	    # --anchor 미지정이면 현행 그대로 각 슬라이스에서 즉시 exit 2 — 지정 시에만
  7042	    # 슬라이스 진단을 모아 마지막에 판정 차분(anchor_diff)으로 exit 를 정한다.
  7043	    collected: list[str] = []
  7044	    pending_analysis: list[str] = []
  7045	    tree_findings, tree_candidates, tree_keys, candidate_keys = _tree_slice2(config.root, bcs)
  7046	    if tree_findings and config.anchor is None:
  7047	        # tree 위반이 code 레인 전에 exit 2 를 선점한다(현행) — code 미실행이라
  7048	        # 선점 억제 없음(tree 단독 그대로).
  7049	        _print_tree_blocks(tree_findings, tree_candidates)
  7050	        return 2
  7051	
  7052	    if config.profile != "auto":
  7053	        try:
  7054	            # tree↔code 동일 사건 선점 억제를 위해 code 레인을 먼저 계산한다 —
  7055	            # 분석 불능(UsageError)이어도 확정 tree 진단·후보는 exit 1 전에 그대로
  7056	            # 인쇄한다(현행 stdout 순서 보존 · 억제는 code 실발화 시에만).
  7057	            try:
  7058	                analysis, findings = _run(config)
  7059	            except UsageError:
  7060	                _print_tree_blocks(tree_findings, tree_candidates)
  7061	                raise
  7062	            dynamic_proof_only = bool(analysis) and all(
  7063	                issue.startswith("DYNAMIC_ERROR_SHAPE_PROOF_REQUIRED ")
  7064	                for issue in analysis
  7065	            )
  7066	            reported_findings = findings
  7067	            if dynamic_proof_only:
  7068	                reported_findings = [
  7069	                    finding
  7070	                    for finding in findings
  7071	                    if not finding.requires_static_error_shape
  7072	                ]
  7073	            emit_code = bool(reported_findings) and (not analysis or dynamic_proof_only)
  7074	            if emit_code and (tree_findings or tree_candidates):
  7075	                code_keys = _code_overlap_keys(reported_findings)
  7076	                tree_findings = _suppress_overlapped_tree(tree_findings, tree_keys, code_keys)
  7077	                tree_candidates = _suppress_overlapped_tree(tree_candidates, candidate_keys, code_keys)
  7078	            _print_tree_blocks(tree_findings, tree_candidates)
  7079	            if tree_findings:
  7080	                collected.extend(lines(tree_findings))
  7081	            if emit_code:
  7082	                print(
  7083	                    "[check-api-error-controller-contract] BLOCKER — code-profile "
  7084	                    "controller error mapping contract violation:"
  7085	                )
  7086	                # 방출 표면 — 라인은 레코드 필드의 순수 함수(violation `[#N] {where}:
  7087	                # {msg}` · 계약 `- {where}: {msg}`)이고, stdout 인쇄 순서 = 레코드
  7088	                # 순서(emit_all 불변식). 판정(#N/계약)이 갈릴 때마다 defer 컬렉션을
  7089	                # 이어 붙여 현행 인쇄 순서를 보존한다.
  7090	                surfaces: "list[Findings | ContractFindings]" = []
  7091	
  7092	                def _violation_surface() -> Findings:
  7093	                    tail = surfaces[-1] if surfaces else None
  7094	                    if not isinstance(tail, Findings):
  7095	                        tail = Findings(defer=True)
  7096	                        surfaces.append(tail)
  7097	                    return tail
  7098	
  7099	                def _contract_surface() -> ContractFindings:
  7100	                    tail = surfaces[-1] if surfaces else None
  7101	                    if not isinstance(tail, ContractFindings):
  7102	                        tail = ContractFindings(CONTRACT_REF, defer=True)
  7103	                        surfaces.append(tail)
  7104	                    return tail
  7105	
  7106	                for finding in reported_findings:
  7107	                    finding_where: str = f"{finding.path}:{finding.lineno}"
  7108	                    finding_msg: str = f"{finding.category}: {finding.shown}"
  7109	                    if finding.rule is not None:
  7110	                        _violation_surface().add(
  7111	                            finding.rule, finding_where, finding_msg, symbol=finding.symbol
  7112	                        )
  7113	                    else:
  7114	                        _contract_surface().add(
  7115	                            where=finding_where, msg=finding_msg, symbol=finding.symbol
  7116	                        )
  7117	                emit_all(*surfaces, printer=print)
  7118	                print(
  7119	                    "  근거: known BC failures are mapped directly by their selected "
  7120	                    "owner controller through one narrow exception or try-free Result "
  7121	                    "path and direct two-argument Status(<approved HTTP status>, error); helper/handler/raw "
  7122	                    "detours are not part of this contract."
  7123	                )
  7124	                if config.anchor is None:
  7125	                    return 2
  7126	                for surface in surfaces:
  7127	                    collected.extend(lines(surface))
  7128	                # 토큰-only analysis 는 차분 강등으로 소거되지 않는다 — findings 가
  7129	                # 전건 앵커 기존분이어도 proof 경로(exit 1)로 남긴다(fail-open 차단).
  7130	                pending_analysis = analysis
  7131	            elif analysis:
  7132	                raise UsageError("; ".join(analysis))
  7133	        except UsageError as exc:
  7134	            print(
  7135	                f"[check-api-error-controller-contract] 사용 오류: {exc}",
  7136	                file=sys.stderr,
  7137	            )
  7138	            return 1
  7139	    else:
  7140	        _print_tree_blocks(tree_findings, tree_candidates)
  7141	        if tree_findings:
  7142	            collected.extend(lines(tree_findings))
  7143	
  7144	    if collected:
  7145	        try:
  7146	            verdict = anchor_diff.partition_exit(
  7147	                script=Path(__file__).resolve(),
  7148	                label="[check-api-error-controller-contract]",
  7149	                target=config.root,
  7150	                anchor=config.anchor or "",
  7151	                argv=argv[1:],
  7152	                findings=collected,
  7153	                path_flags=frozenset({"--api-module", "--controller-module"}),
  7154	                debt_file=config.anchor_debt_file,
  7155	                analysis_pending=bool(pending_analysis),

   656	def _source_plan(
   657	    config: Config, inventory: CodeInventory
   658	) -> tuple[set[Path], set[Path], set[Path], list[str], list[Blocker]]:
   659	    inventory_paths = set(inventory.relative_paths)
   660	    analysis: list[str] = []
   661	    blockers: list[Blocker] = []
   662	    code_paths = {Path(raw) for raw in config.code_error_modules}
   663	    preserve_paths = {Path(raw) for raw in config.preserve_error_modules}
   664	    source_paths: set[Path] = set()
   665	
   666	    common_selected = [Path(config.api_module or "")]
   667	    common_selected.extend(Path(raw) for raw in config.controller_modules)
   668	    common_selected.extend(code_paths)
   669	    common_selected.extend(preserve_paths)
   670	    for path in common_selected:
   671	        if path in inventory_paths:
   672	            source_paths.add(path)
   673	        elif config.profile == "preserve-established" or path not in code_paths:
   674	            analysis.append(f"선택 source가 production inventory에 없음: {path}")
   675	
   676	    if config.profile != "dddjango-code-json":
   677	        return source_paths, code_paths, preserve_paths, analysis, blockers
   678	
   679	    discovered = {path for path in inventory_paths if _is_schema_candidate(path)}
   680	    union = code_paths | preserve_paths
   681	    if COMMON_ERROR not in code_paths:
   682	        analysis.append(
   683	            "code inventory에 framework/ninja/framework_error_schema.py가 필요함"
   684	        )
   685	    for path in union:
   686	        if not _is_schema_candidate(path):
   687	            analysis.append(f"project error inventory의 noncanonical module: {path}")
   688	    if code_paths & preserve_paths:
   689	        analysis.append("project code/preserve error inventory overlap")
   690	
   691	    required_missing = {COMMON_ERROR}
   692	    required_missing.update(_bc_error_path(bc) for bc in config.error_bcs)
   693	    for path in sorted(required_missing, key=Path.as_posix):
   694	        if path not in inventory_paths:
   695	            blockers.append(
   696	                Blocker(
   697	                    BLOCKER_KIND_COMMON if path == COMMON_ERROR else BLOCKER_KIND_BC_ERROR,
   698	                    path,
   699	                    "필수 canonical FrameworkErrorSchema artifact 부재",
   700	                )
   701	            )
   702	    for bc in config.error_bcs:
   703	        path = _bc_error_path(bc)
   704	        if path in inventory_paths and path not in code_paths:
   705	            analysis.append(f"designated error BC module이 code inventory에 없음: {path}")
   706	
   707	    for path in sorted(union - discovered, key=Path.as_posix):
   708	        if path not in required_missing:
   709	            analysis.append(f"project error inventory module이 production candidate에 없음: {path}")
   710	    for path in sorted(discovered - union, key=Path.as_posix):
   711	        if _skeleton_placeholder_module(config.root, path):
   712	            continue  # 렌더 계약이 제외한 빈 골격 — _skeleton_placeholder_module docstring
   713	        analysis.append(f"canonical candidate가 project inventory union에 없음: {path}")
   714	
   715	    for bc in config.scope_bcs:
   716	        scoped = {path for path in inventory_paths if _path_under_bc(path, bc)}
   717	        if not scoped and not config.anchor_baseline:
   718	            # anchor-baseline 모드에선 앵커 트리에 없는 scope BC 가 정상이다 —
   719	            # 그 표면의 진단은 정의상 전건 신규라 기준선에 있을 수 없다.
   720	            analysis.append(f"scope BC production source 없음: application/{bc}/")

  1971	        return True
  1972	    return _static_literal_value(
  1973	        _resolve(value, bindings) if value is not None else None
  1974	    ) is None

  1977	def _node_symbol(node: ast.AST) -> str | None:
  1978	    """위반 심볼(U17) — node 가 안정 이름을 가진 경우만 채우고, 그 밖은 null."""
  1979	    if isinstance(node, (ast.ClassDef, ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
  1980	        return node.name
  1981	    if isinstance(node, ast.AnnAssign) and isinstance(node.target, ast.Name):
  1982	        return node.target.id
  1983	    return None

  1985	
  1986	def _append_finding(
  1987	    findings: list[Finding],
  1988	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  1989	    parsed: ParsedSource,
  1990	    node: ast.AST,
  1991	    category: str,
  1992	    *,
  1993	    rule: str | None = None,
  1994	    requires_static_error_shape: bool = False,
  1995	) -> None:
  1996	    lineno = getattr(node, "lineno", 1)
  1997	    key = (parsed.relative_path, lineno, category)
  1998	    if key in seen:
  1999	        return
  2000	    seen.add(key)
  2001	    source_lines = parsed.source.splitlines()
  2002	    shown = (
  2003	        source_lines[lineno - 1].strip()
  2004	        if 0 < lineno <= len(source_lines)
  2005	        else category
  2006	    )
  2007	    findings.append(
  2008	        Finding(
  2009	            parsed.relative_path,
  2010	            lineno,
  2011	            category,
  2012	            shown,
  2013	            rule=rule,
  2014	            symbol=_node_symbol(node),
  2015	            requires_static_error_shape=requires_static_error_shape,
  2016	        )
  2017	    )

  2019	
  2020	def _base_contract(
  2021	    parsed: ParsedSource,
  2022	    node: ast.ClassDef,
  2023	    bindings: dict[str, str],
  2024	    expected: str,
  2025	    expected_tail: str,
  2026	    analysis: list[str],
  2027	    findings: list[Finding],
  2028	    seen: set[tuple[Path, int, str]],
  2029	    category: str,
  2030	    rule: str | None = None,
  2031	) -> bool:
  2032	    resolved = [_resolve(base, bindings) for base in node.bases]
  2033	    if len(node.bases) == 1 and resolved == [expected]:
  2034	        return True
  2035	    if expected in resolved:
  2036	        _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, node, category, rule=rule)
  2037	        return False
  2038	    raw_tails = [(_expression_name(base) or "").rsplit(".", 1)[-1] for base in node.bases]
  2039	    resolved_tails = [(name or "").rsplit(".", 1)[-1] for name in resolved]
  2040	    if expected_tail in raw_tails or expected_tail in resolved_tails:
  2041	        analysis.append(
  2042	            f"{parsed.relative_path}:{node.lineno} required base provenance 분석 불능: {expected}"
  2043	        )
  2044	    else:
  2045	        _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, node, category, rule=rule)
  2046	    return False
  2047	
  2048	
  2049	def _class_body_bindings(
  2050	    parsed: ParsedSource,
  2051	    node: ast.ClassDef,
  2052	    initial_bindings: dict[str, str],
  2053	) -> dict[int, dict[str, str]]:
  2054	    bindings = dict(initial_bindings)
  2055	    before: dict[int, dict[str, str]] = {}
  2056	    for statement in node.body:
  2057	        before[id(statement)] = dict(bindings)
  2058	        _update_bindings(parsed, statement, bindings, module_scope=False)
  2059	    return before
  2060	
  2061	
  2062	def _public_annassigns(
  2063	    parsed: ParsedSource,
  2064	    node: ast.ClassDef,
  2065	    initial_bindings: dict[str, str],
  2240	    allow_schema_decorators: bool = False,
  2241	    # BC base 주체 전용(귀속 매핑표 v2 행17ⓑ) — base 의 추가 field 사건은 같은 실행의
  2242	    # «…must narrow exactly one common field…»(#572) finding 이 단독 대표하므로
  2243	    # «additional public field» 를 중복 방출하지 않는다(자기 중복 소거).
  2244	    suppress_additional_public_field: bool = False,
  2245	) -> None:
  2246	    def pydantic_hook(name: str) -> bool:
  2247	        return name.startswith(("__get_pydantic_", "__pydantic_")) or name in {
  2248	            "__get_validators__",
  2249	            "__modify_schema__",
  2250	        }
  2251	
  2252	    def contains_schema_decorator_factory(
  2253	        value: ast.AST | None,
  2254	        bindings: dict[str, str],
  2255	    ) -> bool:
  2256	        if value is None:
  2257	            return False
  2258	        for candidate in ast.walk(value):
  2259	            if not isinstance(candidate, ast.Call):
  2260	                continue
  2261	            resolved = _resolve(candidate.func, bindings)
  2262	            if resolved in SCHEMA_DECORATORS:
  2263	                return True
  2264	            if (
  2265	                resolved is not None
  2266	                and resolved.startswith("pydantic.")
  2267	                and resolved.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] in SCHEMA_DECORATOR_TAILS
  2268	            ):
  2269	                return True
  2270	        return False
  2271	
  2272	    if not allow_model_config:
  2273	        for decorator in node.decorator_list:
  2274	            _append_finding(
  2275	                findings,
  2276	                seen,
  2277	                parsed,
  2278	                decorator,
  2279	                "class decorator outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2280	            )
  2281	        for keyword in node.keywords:
  2282	            _append_finding(
  2283	                findings,
  2284	                seen,
  2285	                parsed,
  2286	                keyword,
  2287	                "class keyword config outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2288	            )
  2289	    field_names: set[str] = set()
  2290	    before = _class_body_bindings(parsed, node, initial_bindings)
  2291	    for statement in node.body:
  2292	        bindings = before.get(id(statement), initial_bindings)
  2293	        if isinstance(statement, ast.AnnAssign) and isinstance(statement.target, ast.Name):
  2294	            name = statement.target.id
  2295	            if not allow_model_config and pydantic_hook(name):
  2296	                _append_finding(
  2297	                    findings,
  2298	                    seen,
  2299	                    parsed,
  2300	                    statement,
  2301	                    "Pydantic hook override outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2302	                )
  2303	                continue
  2304	            if name == "model_config":
  2305	                if not allow_model_config:
  2306	                    _append_finding(
  2307	                        findings,
  2308	                        seen,
  2309	                        parsed,
  2310	                        statement,
  2311	                        "model_config override outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2312	                    )
  2313	                continue
  2314	            if _classvar(statement.annotation, bindings):
  2315	                if _classvar_binding_requires_runtime_proof(statement.value):
  2316	                    if allow_model_config:
  2317	                        analysis.append(
  2318	                            "DYNAMIC_ERROR_SHAPE_PROOF_REQUIRED "
  2319	                            f"{parsed.relative_path}:{statement.lineno} "
  2320	                            "common FrameworkErrorSchema dynamic ClassVar binding은 "
  2321	                            "target-pin runtime proof 필요"
  2322	                        )
  2323	                    else:
  2324	                        _append_finding(
  2325	                            findings,
  2326	                            seen,
  2327	                            parsed,
  2328	                            statement,
  2329	                            "dynamic ClassVar assignment outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2330	                        )
  2331	                continue
  2332	            if name.startswith("_"):
  2333	                if not allow_model_config and contains_schema_decorator_factory(
  2334	                    statement.value,
  2335	                    bindings,
  2336	                ):
  2337	                    _append_finding(
  2338	                        findings,
  2339	                        seen,
  2340	                        parsed,
  2341	                        statement,
  2342	                        "schema decorator proxy outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2343	                    )
  2344	                elif (
  2345	                    not allow_model_config
  2346	                    and _static_assignment_binding(statement.value, bindings) is None
  2347	                ):
  2348	                    _append_finding(
  2349	                        findings,
  2350	                        seen,
  2351	                        parsed,
  2352	                        statement,
  2353	                        "dynamic private class assignment outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2354	                    )
  2355	                continue
  2356	            if name in field_names:
  2357	                _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "duplicate public field")
  2358	            field_names.add(name)
  2359	            if name not in allowed_fields and not suppress_additional_public_field:
  2360	                _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "additional public field")
  2361	        elif isinstance(statement, ast.Assign):
  2362	            names = {name for target in statement.targets for name in _target_names(target)}
  2363	            if not names and not allow_model_config:
  2364	                _append_finding(
  2365	                    findings,
  2366	                    seen,
  2367	                    parsed,
  2368	                    statement,
  2369	                    "complex class assignment outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2370	                )
  2371	            for name in names:
  2372	                if not allow_model_config and pydantic_hook(name):
  2373	                    _append_finding(
  2374	                        findings,
  2375	                        seen,
  2376	                        parsed,
  2377	                        statement,
  2378	                        "Pydantic hook override outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2379	                    )
  2380	                elif name == "model_config":
  2381	                    if not allow_model_config:
  2382	                        _append_finding(
  2383	                            findings,
  2384	                            seen,
  2385	                            parsed,
  2386	                            statement,
  2387	                            "model_config override outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2388	                        )
  2389	                elif not name.startswith("_"):
  2390	                    _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "public class assignment/helper")
  2391	                elif not allow_model_config and contains_schema_decorator_factory(
  2392	                    statement.value,
  2393	                    bindings,
  2394	                ):
  2395	                    _append_finding(
  2396	                        findings,
  2397	                        seen,
  2398	                        parsed,
  2399	                        statement,
  2400	                        "schema decorator proxy outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2401	                    )
  2402	            if (
  2403	                not allow_model_config
  2404	                and names
  2405	                and all(name.startswith("_") for name in names)
  2406	                and _static_assignment_binding(statement.value, bindings) is None
  2407	            ):
  2408	                _append_finding(
  2409	                    findings,
  2410	                    seen,
  2411	                    parsed,
  2412	                    statement,
  2413	                    "dynamic private class assignment outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2414	                )
  2415	        elif isinstance(statement, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
  2416	            decorated_schema_method = any(
  2417	                _resolve(
  2418	                    decorator.func if isinstance(decorator, ast.Call) else decorator,
  2419	                    bindings,
  2420	                )
  2421	                in SCHEMA_DECORATORS
  2422	                for decorator in statement.decorator_list
  2423	            )
  2424	            if not allow_schema_decorators or (
  2425	                not decorated_schema_method and not statement.name.startswith("_")
  2426	            ):
  2427	                _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "validator/public helper")
  2428	        elif (
  2429	            isinstance(statement, ast.ClassDef)
  2430	            and not statement.name.startswith("_")
  2431	            and not (allow_model_config and statement.name == "Config")
  2432	        ):
  2433	            _append_finding(findings, seen, parsed, statement, "public nested class/helper")
  2434	        elif (
  2435	            isinstance(statement, ast.ClassDef)
  2436	            and allow_model_config
  2437	            and statement.name == "Config"
  2438	        ) or isinstance(statement, ast.Pass) or (
  2439	            isinstance(statement, ast.Expr)
  2440	            and isinstance(statement.value, ast.Constant)
  2441	            and isinstance(statement.value.value, str)
  2442	        ):
  2443	            continue
  2444	        else:
  2445	            if allow_model_config:
  2446	                analysis.append(
  2447	                    "DYNAMIC_ERROR_SHAPE_PROOF_REQUIRED "
  2448	                    f"{parsed.relative_path}:{statement.lineno} "
  2449	                    "common FrameworkErrorSchema executable class-body statement는 target-pin runtime proof 필요"
  2450	                )
  2451	            else:
  2452	                _append_finding(
  2453	                    findings,
  2454	                    seen,
  2455	                    parsed,
  2456	                    statement,
  2457	                    "executable class-body statement outside common FrameworkErrorSchema",
  2458	                )
  2459	
  2460	
  2461	def _assignment_value(statement: ast.stmt) -> ast.AST | None:
  2462	    if isinstance(statement, ast.Assign):
  2463	        return statement.value
  2464	    if isinstance(statement, ast.AnnAssign):
  2465	        return statement.value
  2466	    return None
  2467	
  2468	
  2469	def _is_functional_enum(
  2470	    statement: ast.stmt, bindings: dict[str, str]
  2471	) -> bool:
  2472	    value = _assignment_value(statement)
  2473	    return (
  2474	        isinstance(value, ast.Call)
  2475	        and _resolve(value.func, bindings) in ENUM_BASES
  2476	    )
